\textbf{\textbf{Abstract}}

analysis is an effective means to make sense of public opinion toward
events, products or organizations{[}1{]}. In the case of electric cars

analysis is an effective means to make sense of public opinion toward
events, products or organizations{[}1{]}. In the case of electric cars
(EC), sentiment analysis can yield useful information to understand how
consumers feel towards EC brands and thus, get a sense of the general
opinions about EC brands{[}2{]}. This research explores the application
of large language models (LLMs), including BERT for sentiment analysis

analysis is an effective means to make sense of public opinion toward

x20techniques.ux20Theseux20resultsux2cux20togetherux20withux20theux20interestux20shownux20byux20industryux20andux20academiaux2cux20suggestux20thatux20thereux20isux20stillux20ampleux20roomux20forux20researchux20opportunitiesux20onux20domainsux2cux20techniquesux20andux20practicalux20applicationsux2cux20andux20weux20expectux20toux20keepux20observingux20aux20sustainedux20cadenceux20inux20theux20numberux20ofux20publishedux20papersux2cux20patentsux20andux20commercialux20toolsux20madeux20available.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Expertux20Systemsux20withux20Applicationsux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.eswa.2023.119862ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux220957-4174ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22Expertux20Systemsux20withux20Applicationsux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux22119862ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22ScienceDirectux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Aux20reviewux20onux20sentimentux20analysisux20fromux20socialux20mediaux20platformsux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.sciencedirect.comux2fscienceux2farticleux2fpiiux2fS0957417423003639ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux22223ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Rodruxedguez-Ibuxe1nezux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Margaritaux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Casuxe1nez-Venturaux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Antonioux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Castejuxf3n-Mateosux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Fuxe9lixux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Cuenca-Jimuxe9nezux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Pedro-Manuelux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c1ux2c5ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222023ux22ux2c8ux2c1ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDMfllEGhUcL}{}{}Sentiment
analysis is an effective means to make sense of public opinion toward
events, products or organizations{[}1{]}. In the case of electric cars
(EC), sentiment analysis can yield useful information to understand how
consumers feel towards EC brands and thus, get a sense of the general
opinions about EC brands{[}2{]}. This research explores the application
of large language models (LLMs), including BERT for sentiment analysis
in the EC domain{[}3{]}.

We developed a sentiment analysis tool for sentiment analysis of public
opinion of electric vehicle by scraping data on various websites viz.,
new article websites, blog sites, forum sites and survey
sites\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22RIh8ym5Sux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d4ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d4ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:402ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fKPMRQCNYux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:402ux2cux22typeux22:ux22webpageux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Sentimentux20Analysisux20ofux20Onlineux20Newux20Energyux20Vehicleux20Reviewsux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.mdpi.comux2f2076-3417ux2f13ux2f14ux2f8176ux22ux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c1ux2c5ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDRzUJV6UINo}{}{}We
developed a sentiment analysis tool for sentiment analysis of public
opinion of electric vehicle by scraping data on various websites viz.,
new article websites, blog sites, forum sites and survey sites{[}4{]}.
This tool incorporates advanced text mining techniques, supporting
multiple languages such as English, German, Chinese, and Norwegian. It
automates the data extraction from a set of designated URLs, applies
text preprocessing with segmentation and stopword removal, and applies
sentiment classification to positive, negative, and neutral
classifications\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22TZn3D7egux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d5ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d5ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:407ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fK6GMA5FMux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:407ux2cux22typeux22:ux22bookux22ux2cux22ISBNux22:ux22978-3-0365-8983-1ux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22noteux22:ux22DOI:ux2010.3390ux2fbooks978-3-0365-8983-1ux22ux2cux22publisherux22:ux22MDPIux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22DOI.orgux20ux28Crossrefux29ux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Newux20Insightsux20inux20Machineux20Learningux20andux20Deepux20Neuralux20Networksux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.mdpi.comux2fbooksux2fbookux2f8315ux22ux2cux22editorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Figueiraux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22uxc1lvaroux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Rennaux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Francescoux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c1ux2c5ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222023ux22ux2c11ux2c24ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDMulKTiRPaS}{}{}We
developed a sentiment analysis tool for sentiment analysis of public
opinion of electric vehicle by scraping data on various websites viz.,
new article websites, blog sites, forum sites and survey
sites\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22RIh8ym5Sux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d4ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d4ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:402ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fKPMRQCNYux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:402ux2cux22typeux22:ux22webpageux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Sentimentux20Analysisux20ofux20Onlineux20Newux20Energyux20Vehicleux20Reviewsux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.mdpi.comux2f2076-3417ux2f13ux2f14ux2f8176ux22ux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c1ux2c5ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDRzUJV6UINo}{}{}We
developed a sentiment analysis tool for sentiment analysis of public
opinion of electric vehicle by scraping data on various websites viz.,
new article websites, blog sites, forum sites and survey sites{[}4{]}.
This tool incorporates advanced text mining techniques, supporting
multiple languages such as English, German, Chinese, and Norwegian. It
automates the data extraction from a set of designated URLs, applies
text preprocessing with segmentation and stopword removal, and applies
sentiment classification to positive, negative, and neutral
classifications{[}5{]}. The tool also includes a comprehensive survey
analysis feature, enabling the generation of questions, sentiment
classification of responses, and visualization of sentiment
distributions. By utilizing transformer models for survey generation and
review analysis, the tool provides with actionable insights into
consumer attitudes and brand perception, making it a valuable resource
for market research and opinion analysis in the rapidly growing EC
sector\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22RX1K8Talux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d6ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d6ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:408ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fKN6454IQux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:408ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Sentimentux20analysisux20hasux20comeux20longux20wayux20sinceux20itux20wasux20introducedux20asux20aux20naturalux20languageux20processingux20taskux20nearlyux2020ux20yearsux20ago.ux20Sentimentux20analysisux20aimsux20toux20extractux20theux20underlyingux20attitudesux20andux20opinionsux20towardux20anux20entity.ux20Itux20hasux20becomeux20aux20powerfulux20toolux20usedux20byux20governmentsux2cux20businessesux2cux20medicineux2cux20marketingux2cux20andux20others.ux20Theux20traditionalux20sentimentux20analysisux20modelux20focusesux20mainlyux20onux20textux20content.ux20Howeverux2cux20technologicalux20advancesux20haveux20allowedux20peopleux20toux20expressux20theirux20opinionsux20andux20feelingsux20throughux20audioux2cux20imageux20andux20videoux20channels.ux20Asux20aux20resultux2cux20sentimentux20analysisux20isux20shiftingux20fromux20unimodalityux20toux20multimodality.ux20Multimodalux20sentimentux20analysisux20bringsux20newux20opportunitiesux20withux20theux20rapidux20increaseux20ofux20sentimentux20analysisux20asux20complementaryux20dataux20streamsux20enableux20improvedux20andux20deeperux20sentimentux20detectionux20whichux20goesux20beyondux20text-basedux20analysis.ux20Audioux20andux20videoux20channelsux20areux20includedux20inux20multimodalux20sentimentux20analysisux20inux20termsux20ofux20broadness.ux20Peopleux20haveux20beenux20workingux20onux20differentux20approachesux20toux20improveux20sentimentux20analysisux20systemux20performanceux20byux20employingux20complexux20deepux20neuralux20architectures.ux20Recentlyux2cux20sentimentux20analysisux20hasux20achievedux20significantux20successux20usingux20theux20transformer-basedux20model.ux20Thisux20paperux20presentsux20aux20comprehensiveux20studyux20ofux20differentux20sentimentux20analysisux20approachesux2cux20applicationsux2cux20challengesux2cux20andux20resourcesux20thenux20concludesux20thatux20itux20holdsux20tremendousux20potential.ux20Theux20primaryux20motivationux20ofux20thisux20surveyux20isux20toux20highlightux20changingux20trendsux20inux20theux20unimodalityux20toux20multimodalityux20forux20solvingux20sentimentux20analysisux20tasks.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22ACMux20Comput.ux20Surv.ux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1145ux2f3586075ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux220360-0300ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux2213sux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux22270:1ux2013270:38ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22ACMux20Digitalux20Libraryux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Multimodalux20Sentimentux20Analysis:ux20Aux20Surveyux20ofux20Methodsux2cux20Trendsux2cux20andux20Challengesux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Multimodalux20Sentimentux20Analysisux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fdoi.orgux2f10.1145ux2f3586075ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux2255ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Dasux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ringkiux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Singhux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Thoudamux20Dorenux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c1ux2c5ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222023ux22ux2c7ux2c13ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDUJzT8oC9cN}{}{}We

new article websites, blog sites, forum sites and survey

sites\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22RIh8ym5Sux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d4ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d4ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:402ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fKPMRQCNYux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:402ux2cux22typeux22:ux22webpageux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Sentimentux20Analysisux20ofux20Onlineux20Newux20Energyux20Vehicleux20Reviewsux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.mdpi.comux2f2076-3417ux2f13ux2f14ux2f8176ux22ux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c1ux2c5ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDRzUJV6UINo}{}{}We
developed a sentiment analysis tool for sentiment analysis of public
opinion of electric vehicle by scraping data on various websites viz.,
new article websites, blog sites, forum sites and survey sites{[}4{]}.
This tool incorporates advanced text mining techniques, supporting
multiple languages such as English, German, Chinese, and Norwegian. It
automates the data extraction from a set of designated URLs, applies
text preprocessing with segmentation and stopword removal, and applies
sentiment classification to positive, negative, and neutral
classifications{[}5{]}. The tool also includes a comprehensive survey
analysis feature, enabling the generation of questions, sentiment
classification of responses, and visualization of sentiment
distributions. By utilizing transformer models for survey generation and
review analysis, the tool provides with actionable insights into
consumer attitudes and brand perception, making it a valuable resource
for market research and opinion analysis in the rapidly growing EC
sector{[}6{]}.

Sentiment analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events,
issues, and products. It falls under the domains of machine learning,
data mining, and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back
to the 1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of
social media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer
feedback, SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux223r4NAUKFux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d7ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d7ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:412ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fGAX4SSRZux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:412ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Telematicsux20andux20Informaticsux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.tele.2018.03.019ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux2207365853ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux225ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22Telematicsux20andux20Informaticsux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux221512-1523ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22DOI.orgux20ux28Crossrefux29ux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Whatux20drivesux20purchaseux20intentionux20onux20Airbnbux3fux20Perspectivesux20ofux20consumerux20reviewsux2cux20informationux20qualityux2cux20andux20mediaux20richnessux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Whatux20drivesux20purchaseux20intentionux20onux20Airbnbux3fux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2flinkinghub.elsevier.comux2fretrieveux2fpiiux2fS0736585318300248ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux2235ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Chenux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Chia-Chenux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Changux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ya-Chingux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222018ux22ux2c8ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDCffCLraJgH}{}{}Sentiment
analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events, issues, and
products. It falls under the domains of machine learning, data mining,
and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back to the
1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of social
media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer feedback,
SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations{[}7{]}. This analysis not only provides an insight into the
consumer demand for electric vehicles but also draws attention to the
importance that demographic factors play in influencing views related to
electric vehicles on the

analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events, issues, and
products. It falls under the domains of machine learning, data mining,
and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back to the
1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of social
media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer feedback,
SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations{[}7{]}. This analysis not only provides an insight into the
consumer demand for electric vehicles but also draws attention to the
importance that demographic factors play in influencing views related to
electric vehicles on the subject{[}8{]}. As the demands in the electric
vehicle market rises, the sentiment analysis will play a increasingly
critical role in both the development of marketing strategies and
product development and also the planning of infrastructure for electric

analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events, issues, and
products. It falls under the domains of machine learning, data mining,
and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back to the
1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of social
media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer feedback,
SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux223r4NAUKFux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d7ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d7ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:412ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fGAX4SSRZux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:412ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Telematicsux20andux20Informaticsux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.tele.2018.03.019ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux2207365853ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux225ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22Telematicsux20andux20Informaticsux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux221512-1523ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22DOI.orgux20ux28Crossrefux29ux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Whatux20drivesux20purchaseux20intentionux20onux20Airbnbux3fux20Perspectivesux20ofux20consumerux20reviewsux2cux20informationux20qualityux2cux20andux20mediaux20richnessux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Whatux20drivesux20purchaseux20intentionux20onux20Airbnbux3fux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2flinkinghub.elsevier.comux2fretrieveux2fpiiux2fS0736585318300248ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux2235ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Chenux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Chia-Chenux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Changux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ya-Chingux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222018ux22ux2c8ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDCffCLraJgH}{}{}Sentiment
analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events, issues, and
products. It falls under the domains of machine learning, data mining,
and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back to the
1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of social
media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer feedback,
SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations{[}7{]}. This analysis not only provides an insight into the
consumer demand for electric vehicles but also draws attention to the
importance that demographic factors play in influencing views related to
electric vehicles on the subject{[}8{]}. As the demands in the electric
vehicle market rises, the sentiment analysis will play a increasingly
critical role in both the development of marketing strategies and
product development and also the planning of infrastructure for electric
cars{[}9{]}. With the application of advanced analytical techniques it
will be possible to gather information about consumer behavior which
will allow stakeholders to make rapid and appropriate responses to
changes that occur in the

analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events, issues, and
products. It falls under the domains of machine learning, data mining,
and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back to the
1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of social
media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer feedback,
SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations{[}7{]}. This analysis not only provides an insight into the
consumer demand for electric vehicles but also draws attention to the
importance that demographic factors play in influencing views related to
electric vehicles on the
subject\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux229j3yXRpoux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d8ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d8ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:413ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fXGCZ3P7Sux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:413ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Electricux20vehiclesux20haveux20gainedux20significantux20popularityux20inux20theux20marketux2cux20withux20salesux20increasingux20yearly.ux20Theux20introductionux20ofux20newux20policiesux20andux20reformsux20aimedux20atux20promotingux20environmentalux20sustainabilityux2cux20coupledux20withux20theux20releaseux20ofux20moreux20advancedux20electricux20vehiclesux20withux20higherux20drivingux20rangesux20andux20technicalux20specificationsux2cux20hasux20encouragedux20moreux20peopleux20toux20considerux20switchingux20toux20electricux20vehicles.ux20Howeverux2cux20thereux20isux20stillux20aux20lackux20ofux20understandingux20ofux20publicux20perceptionux20andux20theux20factorsux20influencingux20theux20decisionux20toux20switchux20toux20electricux20vehiclesux2cux20especiallyux20amongux20peopleux20fromux20differentux20demographicux20groups.ux20Inux20thisux20studyux2cux20weux20leverageux20machineux20learningux20techniquesux20toux20analyzeux20publicux20opinionux20aboutux20electricux20vehiclesux20acrossux20differentux20demographicux20groupsux20onux20twoux20onlineux20socialux20networksux20ux28OSNsux29ux2cux20namelyux20Redditux20andux20Twitter.ux20Ourux20analysesux20provideux20valuableux20insightsux20intoux20howux20usersux20onux20theseux20platformsux20perceiveux20electricux20vehiclesux20andux20theux20factorsux20thatux20influenceux20theirux20perception.ux20Thisux20informationux20canux20beux20usedux20toux20informux20marketux20strategiesux20 analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events, issues, and
products. It falls under the domains of machine learning, data mining,
and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back to the
1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of social
media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer feedback,
SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux223r4NAUKFux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d7ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d7ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:412ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fGAX4SSRZux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:412ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Telematicsux20andux20Informaticsux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.tele.2018.03.019ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux2207365853ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux225ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22Telematicsux20andux20Informaticsux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux221512-1523ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22DOI.orgux20ux28Crossrefux29ux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Whatux20drivesux20purchaseux20intentionux20onux20Airbnbux3fux20Perspectivesux20ofux20consumerux20reviewsux2cux20informationux20qualityux2cux20andux20mediaux20richnessux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Whatux20drivesux20purchaseux20intentionux20onux20Airbnbux3fux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2flinkinghub.elsevier.comux2fretrieveux2fpiiux2fS0736585318300248ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux2235ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Chenux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Chia-Chenux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Changux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ya-Chingux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222018ux22ux2c8ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDCffCLraJgH}{}{}Sentiment
analysis (SA) seeks to evaluate public opinion on events, issues, and
products. It falls under the domains of machine learning, data mining,
and natural language processing (NLP). While NLP dates back to the
1950s, SA gained significant attention in 2005 with the rise of social
media and the abundance of textual data. By analyzing customer feedback,
SA enables businesses to gain valuable insights into their
operations{[}7{]}. This analysis not only provides an insight into the
consumer demand for electric vehicles but also draws attention to the
importance that demographic factors play in influencing views related to
electric vehicles on the subject{[}8{]}. As the demands in the electric
vehicle market rises, the sentiment analysis will play a increasingly
critical role in both the development of marketing strategies and
product development and also the planning of infrastructure for electric
cars{[}9{]}. With the application of advanced analytical techniques it
will be possible to gather information about consumer behavior which
will allow stakeholders to make rapid and appropriate responses to
changes that occur in the market{[}10{]}.

Keywords

Sentiment Analysis (SA), Natural Language Processing (NLP), Electric
Cars (EC), Transformers, User Generated Content (UGC)

transport sector faces mounting scrutiny due to its significant
contribution to environmental and climate challenges{[}11{]}. The issue
of climate change, fuel prices, and battery technology has done a lot
for bringing into the limelight electric cars (EC) in recent
times{[}12{]}. Governments are stimulating EC transition worldwide by
subsidizing, providing infrastructure, and enacting stricter emission
regulations{[}13{]}. But what largely determines the market uptake for
ECs is public perception. Factors influencing the consumers' attitudes
towards ECs are charging infrastructure, driving range, cost, and
environmental

2cux22givenux22:ux22Johnux20Otuux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Khoshnevisux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Pegahux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222024ux22ux2c3ux2c1ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RND4zWVMTm2NK}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-7}{}{}The
transport sector faces mounting scrutiny due to its significant
contribution to environmental and climate challenges{[}11{]}. The issue
of climate change, fuel prices, and battery technology has done a lot
for bringing into the limelight electric cars (EC) in recent
times{[}12{]}. Governments are stimulating EC transition worldwide by
subsidizing, providing infrastructure, and enacting stricter emission
regulations{[}13{]}. But what largely determines the market uptake for
ECs is public perception. Factors influencing the consumers' attitudes
towards ECs are charging infrastructure, driving range, cost, and
environmental benefits{[}14{]}. Sentiment analysis is a data-driven
technique to analyze these perceptions, which would assist stakeholders
in making informed decisions regarding EC policies and marketing

transport sector faces mounting scrutiny due to its significant
contribution to environmental and climate challenges{[}11{]}. The issue
of climate change, fuel prices, and battery technology has done a lot
for bringing into the limelight electric cars (EC) in recent
times{[}12{]}. Governments are stimulating EC transition worldwide by
subsidizing, providing infrastructure, and enacting stricter emission
regulations{[}13{]}. But what largely determines the market uptake for
ECs is public perception. Factors influencing the consumers' attitudes
towards ECs are charging infrastructure, driving range, cost, and
environmental

20usedux20toux20measureux20theux20environmentalux20performanceux20withinux20theux20transportux20sector.ux20Thisux20researchux20considersux20186ux20articlesux20fromux2046ux20journals.ux20Theux20surveyux27sux20mainux20findingsux20showux20theux20ever-increasingux20attentionux20paidux20toux20studyingux20theux20transportux20sectorux27sux20emissionsux2cux20emphasisingux20roadux20andux20passengerux20carux20CO2ux20emissionsux20asux20theux20majorux20sourceux20ofux20emissionsux20inux20theux20transportux20sector.ux20Inux20theux20existingux20literatureux2cux20theux20topux20threeux20frequentlyux20adoptedux20methodologiesux20forux20measuringux20environmentalux20performanceux20inux20transportationux20includeux20Dataux20Envelopmentux20Analysisux2cux20emissionux20analysisux2cux20andux20simulation.ux20Thisux20studyux20showsux20researchux20gapsux20andux20futureux20directionsux20onux20environmentalux20performanceux20assessmentux20withinux20theux20transportux20sectorux2cux20particularlyux20maritimeux20andux20aviation.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Computersux20ux5cux26ux20Industrialux20Engineeringux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.cie.2024.109968ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux220360-8352ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22Computersux20ux5cux26ux20Industrialux20Engineeringux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux22109968ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22ScienceDirectux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Environmentalux20performanceux20assessmentux20inux20theux20transportux20sectorux20usingux20nonparametricux20frontierux20analysis:ux20Aux20systematicux20literatureux20reviewux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Environmentalux20performanceux20assessmentux20inux20theux20transportux20sectorux20usingux20nonparametricux20frontierux20analysisux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.sciencedirect.comux2fscienceux2farticleux2fpiiux2fS0360835224000895ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux22189ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Hatami-Marbiniux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Adelux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Asuux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Johnux20Otuux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Khoshnevisux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Pegahux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222024ux22ux2c3ux2c1ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RND4zWVMTm2NK}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-7}{}{}The
transport sector faces mounting scrutiny due to its significant
contribution to environmental and climate challenges{[}11{]}. The issue
of climate change, fuel prices, and battery technology has done a lot
for bringing into the limelight electric cars (EC) in recent
times{[}12{]}. Governments are stimulating EC transition worldwide by
subsidizing, providing infrastructure, and enacting stricter emission
regulations{[}13{]}. But what largely determines the market uptake for
ECs is public perception. Factors influencing the consumers' attitudes
towards ECs are charging infrastructure, driving range, cost, and
environmental benefits{[}14{]}. Sentiment analysis is a data-driven
technique to analyze these perceptions, which would assist stakeholders
in making informed decisions regarding EC policies and marketing
strategies{[}15{]}.

Sentiment analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP)
techniques, whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are
traded with powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa,
and XLNet for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars
(EVs). Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared
variables and response biases, SA of user-generated content from social
media and online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux2251W0pc1qux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fLPCU9C5Rux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22typeux22:ux22paper-conferenceux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Socialux20Mediaux20prevailsux20inux20todayux27sux20worldux20forux20voicingux20anux20opinion.ux20Thatux20makesux20itux20crucialux20forux20businessesux2cux20artistsux2cux20contentux20creatorsux2cux20andux20prettyux20muchux20anyoneux20elseux20onux20theux20internetux20toux20analyseux20whatux20peopleux20areux20sayingux20aboutux20themux20andux20whatux20theyux20offer.ux20Itux20providesux20vitalux20informationux20onux20whatux20theyux20canux20changeux20andux20improveux20upon.ux20Henceux2cux20Sentimentux20Analysisux20isux20goingux20toux20beux20aux20significantux20aspectux20toux20helpux20organizationsux20andux20individualsux20grow.ux20Contraryux20toux20previousux20workux2cux20aux20broaderux20viewpointux20isux20takenux20intoux20considerationux20andux20anyux20kindux20ofux20discrepanciesux20isux20eliminatedux20sinceux20theux20sentimentux20classifiedux20byux20anyux20oneux20modalityux20isux20confirmedux20byux20theux20analysisux20ofux20theux20otherux20twoux20modalitiesux20asux20well.ux20Inux20allux20theux20previousux20workux20doneux20onux20multimodalux20sentimentux20analysisux2cux20theux20weightageux20givenux20toux20allux20theux20modulesux20wasux20theux20sameux20forux20fusionux20butux20toux20getux20perfectux20resultsux2cux20aux20trial-and-errorux20loggingux20methodux20isux20usedux20withux20manyux20differentux20weightagesux20forux20fusion.ux20Thisux20helpedux20usux20inux20knowingux20whichux20moduleux20isux20bestux20suitedu analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP) techniques,
whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are traded with
powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa, and XLNet
for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars (EVs).
Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and
online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously{[}16{]}. Studies have shown that transformer-based models
significantly outperform lexicon-based approaches with their ability to
assign meaning to nuances in language, including sarcasm and
domain-specific

22Vrundaux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222021ux22ux2c2ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDJPykstZKKu}{}{}Sentiment
analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP) techniques,
whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are traded with
powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa, and XLNet
for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars (EVs).
Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and
online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously{[}16{]}. Studies have shown that transformer-based models
significantly outperform lexicon-based approaches with their ability to
assign meaning to nuances in language, including sarcasm and
domain-specific terminology{[}17{]} . Besides that, range prediction in
these models has been shown to greatly improve performance to capture
shifting consumer concerns regarding affordability and infrastructure
development when tuned into e-car specific data

22Vrundaux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222021ux22ux2c2ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDJPykstZKKu}{}{}Sentiment
analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP) techniques,
whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are traded with
powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa, and XLNet
for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars (EVs).
Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and
online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously{[}16{]}. Studies have shown that transformer-based models
significantly outperform lexicon-based approaches with their ability to
assign meaning to nuances in language, including sarcasm and
domain-specific terminology{[}17{]} . Besides that, range prediction in
these models has been shown to greatly improve performance to capture
shifting consumer concerns regarding affordability and infrastructure
development when tuned into e-car specific data {[}18{]}. This gives
advantage of making data-driven decisions at speed and renders SA as
well obligatory through these to monitor public opinion in the
developing electric car

analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP) techniques,
whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are traded with
powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa, and XLNet
for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars (EVs).
Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and
online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux2251W0pc1qux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fLPCU9C5Rux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22typeux22:ux22paper-conferenceux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Socialux20Mediaux20prevailsux20inux20todayux27sux20worldux20forux20voicingux20anux20opinion.ux20Thatux20makesux20itux20crucialux20forux20businessesux2cux20artistsux2cux20contentux20creatorsux2cux20andux20prettyux20muchux20anyoneux20elseux20onux20theux20internetux20toux20analyseux20whatux20peopleux20areux20sayingux20aboutux20themux20andux20whatux20theyux20offer.ux20Itux20providesux20vitalux20informationux20onux20whatux20theyux20canux20changeux20andux20improveux20upon.ux20Henceux2cux20Sentimentux20Analysisux20isux20goingux20toux20beux20aux20significantux20aspectux20toux20helpux20organizationsux20andux20individualsux20grow.ux20Contraryux20toux20previousux20workux2cux20aux20broaderux20viewpointux20isux20takenux20intoux20considerationux20andux20anyux20kindux20ofux20discrepanciesux20isux20eliminatedux20sinceux20theux20sentimentux20classifiedux20byux20anyux20oneux20modalityux20isux20confirmedux20byux20theux20analysisux20ofux20theux20otherux20twoux20modalitiesux20asux20well.ux20Inux20allux20theux20previousux20workux20doneux20onux20multimodalux20sentimentux20analysisux2cux20theux20weightageux20givenux20toux20allux20theux20modulesux20wasux20theux20sameux20forux20fusionux20butux20toux20getux20perfectux20resultsux2cux20aux20trial-and-errorux20loggingux20methodux20isux20usedux20withux20manyux20differentux20weightagesux20forux20fusion.ux20Thisux20helpedux20usux20inux20knowingux20whichux20moduleux20isux20bestux20suitedu analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP) techniques,
whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are traded with
powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa, and XLNet
for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars (EVs).
Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and
online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously{[}16{]}. Studies have shown that transformer-based models
significantly outperform lexicon-based approaches with their ability to
assign meaning to nuances in language, including sarcasm and
domain-specific

Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and

spontaneously\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux2251W0pc1qux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fLPCU9C5Rux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22typeux22:ux22paper-conferenceux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Socialux20Mediaux20prevailsux20inux20todayux27sux20worldux20forux20voicingux20anux20opinion.ux20Thatux20makesux20itux20crucialux20forux20businessesux2cux20artistsux2cux20contentux20creatorsux2cux20andux20prettyux20muchux20anyoneux20elseux20onux20theux20internetux20toux20analyseux20whatux20peopleux20areux20sayingux20aboutux20themux20andux20whatux20theyux20offer.ux20Itux20providesux20vitalux20informationux20onux20whatux20theyux20canux20changeux20andux20improveux20upon.ux20Henceux2cux20Sentimentux20Analysisux20isux20goingux20toux20beux20aux20significantux20aspectux20toux20helpux20organizationsux20andux20individualsux20grow.ux20Contraryux20toux20previousux20workux2cux20aux20broaderux20viewpointux20isux20takenux20intoux20considerationux20andux20anyux20kindux20ofux20discrepanciesux20isux20eliminatedux20sinceux20theux20sentimentux20classifiedux20byux20anyux20oneux20modalityux20isux20confirmedux20byux20theux20analysisux20ofux20theux20otherux20twoux20modalitiesux20asux20well.ux20Inux20allux20theux20previousux20workux20doneux20onux20multimodalux20sentimentux20analysisux2cux20theux20weightageux20givenux20toux20allux20theux20modulesux20wasux20theux20sameux20forux20fusionux20butux20toux20getux20perfectux20resultsux2cux20aux20trial-and-errorux20loggingux20methodux20isux20usedux20withux20manyux20differentux20weightagesux20forux20fusion.ux20Thisux20helpedux20usux20inux20knowingux20whichux20moduleux20isux20bestux20suitedu 22Vrundaux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222021ux22ux2c2ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDJPykstZKKu}{}{}Sentiment
analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP) techniques,
whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are traded with
powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa, and XLNet
for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars (EVs).
Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and
online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously{[}16{]}. Studies have shown that transformer-based models
significantly outperform lexicon-based approaches with their ability to
assign meaning to nuances in language, including sarcasm and
domain-specific terminology{[}17{]} . Besides that, range prediction in
these models has been shown to greatly improve performance to capture
shifting consumer concerns regarding affordability and infrastructure
development when tuned into e-car specific data

spontaneously\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux2251W0pc1qux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d16ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fLPCU9C5Rux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:442ux2cux22typeux22:ux22paper-conferenceux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Socialux20Mediaux20prevailsux20inux20todayux27sux20worldux20forux20voicingux20anux20opinion.ux20Thatux20makesux20itux20crucialux20forux20businessesux2cux20artistsux2cux20contentux20creatorsux2cux20andux20prettyux20muchux20anyoneux20elseux20onux20theux20internetux20toux20analyseux20whatux20peopleux20areux20sayingux20aboutux20themux20andux20whatux20theyux20offer.ux20Itux20providesux20vitalux20informationux20onux20whatux20theyux20canux20changeux20andux20improveux20upon.ux20Henceux2cux20Sentimentux20Analysisux20isux20goingux20toux20beux20aux20significantux20aspectux20toux20helpux20organizationsux20andux20individualsux20grow.ux20Contraryux20toux20previousux20workux2cux20aux20broaderux20viewpointux20isux20takenux20intoux20considerationux20andux20anyux20kindux20ofux20discrepanciesux20isux20eliminatedux20sinceux20theux20sentimentux20classifiedux20byux20anyux20oneux20modalityux20isux20confirmedux20byux20theux20analysisux20ofux20theux20otherux20twoux20modalitiesux20asux20well.ux20Inux20allux20theux20previousux20workux20doneux20onux20multimodalux20sentimentux20analysisux2cux20theux20weightageux20givenux20toux20allux20theux20modulesux20wasux20theux20sameux20forux20fusionux20butux20toux20getux20perfectux20resultsux2cux20aux20trial-and-errorux20loggingux20methodux20isux20usedux20withux20manyux20differentux20weightagesux20forux20fusion.ux20Thisux20helpedux20usux20inux20knowingux20whichux20moduleux20isux20bestux20suitedux20forux20sentimentalux20analysisux20andux20howux20eachux20ofux20themux20canux20alterux20theux20resultsux20obtained.ux20Withux20thisux20typeux20ofux20theux20developmentux20inux20theux20sentimentux20analysisux20domainux2cux20aux20systemux20whichux20canux20extractux20theux20informationux20inux20termsux20ofux20theux20emotionsux20ofux20theux20peopleux20regardingux20aux20specificux20productux20fromux20anyux20ofux20theux20threeux20mediumsux20ux28textux2cux20audioux20orux20videoux29ux20canux20leadux20toux20theux20betterux20technologicalux20aspectsux20inux20theux20market.ux20Theux20objectiveux20wasux20toux20buildux20aux20systemux20whichux20canux20identifyux20theux20sentimentux20categorizedux20intoux20sixux20types:ux20angerux2cux20joyux2cux20disgustux2cux20sadnessux2cux20fearux2cux20andux20surpriseux20ofux20aux20videoux20whenux20theux20dataux20isux20fedux20intoux20it.ux20Theux20systemux20developedux20depictsux20howux20muchux20ofux20eachux20ofux20theseux20sentimentsux20areux20presentux20inux20aux20particularux20input.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux222021ux20Internationalux20Conferenceux20onux20Computingux2cux20Communicationux2cux20andux20Intelligentux20Systemsux20ux28ICCCISux29ux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1109ux2fICCCIS51004.2021.9397147ux22ux2cux22event-titleux22:ux222021ux20Internationalux20Conferenceux20onux20Computingux2cux20Communicationux2cux20andux20Intelligentux20Systemsux20ux28ICCCISux29ux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux2224-28ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22IEEEux20Xploreux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Sentimentux20Analysisux20onux20User-generatedux20Videoux2cux20Audioux20andux20Textux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fieeexplore.ieee.orgux2fdocumentux2f9397147ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Raoux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ashwiniux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Ahujaux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Akritiux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Kansaraux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Shyamux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Patelux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Vrundaux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222021ux22ux2c2ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDJPykstZKKu}{}{}Sentiment
analysis employs advanced natural language processing (NLP) techniques,
whereby other conventional survey-based frameworks are traded with
powerful large language models (LLMs) such as BERT, RoBERTa, and XLNet
for understanding consumer sentiments regarding electric cars (EVs).
Unlike surveys, which are limited by a set of pre-declared variables and
response biases, SA of user-generated content from social media and
online forums captures real-time consumer opinions at scale,
spontaneously{[}16{]}. Studies have shown that transformer-based models
significantly outperform lexicon-based approaches with their ability to
assign meaning to nuances in language, including sarcasm and
domain-specific terminology{[}17{]} . Besides that, range prediction in
these models has been shown to greatly improve performance to capture
shifting consumer concerns regarding affordability and infrastructure
development when tuned into e-car specific data {[}18{]}. This gives
advantage of making data-driven decisions at speed and renders SA as
well obligatory through these to monitor public opinion in the
developing electric car market{[}19{]}.

This study explores the application of sentiment analysis of electric
cars in three key markets: Germany, China and Norway. The goal is to
assess consumer perceptions of electric cars, evaluate the effectiveness
of transformer-based models, and provide actionable insights. By
leveraging state-of-the-art models, this research contributes to
understanding how advanced sentiment analysis can support the electric
car transition and inform strategies for overcoming barriers to
adoption\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux223m5GzaqMux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d20ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d20ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:451ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2f3FELNE99ux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:451ux2cux22typeux22:ux22webpageux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Mitigatingux20barriersux20toux20adoptingux20electricux20vehiclesux20inux20anux20emergingux20economyux20contextux20-ux20ScienceDirectux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.sciencedirect.comux2fscienceux2farticleux2fpiiux2fS0959652623017158ux22ux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDs7GgIqUVn6}{}{}This
study explores the application of sentiment analysis of electric cars in
three key markets: Germany, China and Norway. The goal is to assess
consumer perceptions of electric cars, evaluate the effectiveness of
transformer-based models, and provide actionable insights. By leveraging
state-of-the-art models, this research contributes to understanding how
advanced sentiment analysis can support the electric car transition and
inform strategies for overcoming barriers to adoption{[}20{]}.

\item

  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-8}{}{}Background
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-9}{}{}In this era of increasing reliance and
dependence on digital communication, online user-generated content (UGC)
has turned into a precious resource for consumer sentiment analysis.

insights into public opinion about

this era of increasing reliance and dependence on digital communication,
online user-generated content (UGC) has turned into a precious resource
for consumer sentiment analysis. Social media, blogs, forums, and news
articles provide real-time insights into public opinion about
e-cars{[}21{]}. The global transition to sustainable transportation has
intensified due to the urgent need to reduce greenhouse gas emissions
and mitigate climate change. Electric cars (ECs) have emerged as a
viable alternative to traditional fossil-fuel vehicles, offering lower
carbon footprints and increased energy efficiency{[}22{]}. Government
and organizations are promoting the electric car adoption by providing
lots of incentives in the form of tax credits, infrastructure investment
and imposing mandates{[}23{]}. Despite these efforts the adoption of
electric cars varies due to economic, infrastructural, and psychological

tyle-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDSZv80FIUiy}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-9}{}{}In
this era of increasing reliance and dependence on digital communication,
online user-generated content (UGC) has turned into a precious resource
for consumer sentiment analysis. Social media, blogs, forums, and news
articles provide real-time insights into public opinion about
e-cars{[}21{]}. The global transition to sustainable transportation has
intensified due to the urgent need to reduce greenhouse gas emissions
and mitigate climate change. Electric cars (ECs) have emerged as a
viable alternative to traditional fossil-fuel vehicles, offering lower
carbon footprints and increased energy efficiency{[}22{]}. Government
and organizations are promoting the electric car adoption by providing
lots of incentives in the form of tax credits, infrastructure investment
and imposing mandates{[}23{]}. Despite these efforts the adoption of
electric cars varies due to economic, infrastructural, and psychological
factors{[}24{]}.

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-10}{}{}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-11}{}{}One of the key factor in the
acceleration of electric car adoption is understanding the consumer
sentiments. Studies suggests that the potential car buyer relies on the

aer19020055ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux220718-1876ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux222ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22JTAERux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22licenseux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fcreativecommons.orgux2flicensesux2fbyux2f4.0ux2fux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux221060-1078ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22DOI.orgux20ux28Crossrefux29ux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Theux20Impactux20ofux20Onlineux20Reviewsux20onux20Consumersux2019ux20Purchaseux20Intentions:ux20Examiningux20theux20Socialux20Influenceux20ofux20Onlineux20Reviewsux2cux20Groupux20Similarityux2cux20andux20Self-Construalux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Theux20Impactux20ofux20Onlineux20Reviewsux20onux20Consumersux2019ux20Purchaseux20Intentionsux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.mdpi.comux2f0718-1876ux2f19ux2f2ux2f55ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux2219ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Ahnux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Yunjeongux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Leeux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Jieunux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c10ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222024ux22ux2c5ux2c10ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RND5VquNcYFmq}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-11}{}{}One
of the key factor in the acceleration of electric car adoption is
understanding the consumer sentiments. Studies suggests that the
potential car buyer relies on the peer reviews, expert opnions and
conversation in the social media platforms{[}25{]}. With new
advancements in the field of AI and natural language processing makes
the sentiment analysis of the user generated text efficient. With the
application of advanced sentiment analysis on social media, news
platforms, and online forums, we can extract real-time insights into
consumer attitudes toward the electric car adoption{[}26{]}. These
insights are crucial for policymakers, manufacturers, and marketers
seeking to address public concerns, refine communication strategies, and
accelerate the transition to electric mobility.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-12}{}{}Motivation
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-13}{}{}The transition toward the sustainable
transportation has increased the need of understanding the consumer
opinion towards electric cars. This study aims at applying the sentiment
analysis to identify the gaps that needs to be bridged for adoption of
electric cars. We will analyze the user-generated content from three
countries---Germany, China, and Norway---to gain insight into the public
sentiment related to electric cars and behavioral trends with regards to
them. The chosen regions present significant contrast when it comes to
adoption rates of electric cars. Norway remains on top with the highest
market penetration, with about 95\% of the new car sales already done in
2023. In China, electric car adoption had soared high, more than 40\% of
the market share by early 2024. Germany had witnessed ups and downs,
with electric cars making 30\% in 2022
\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22QYLqr4ZCux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d27ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d27ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:470ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fPYB6MMC9ux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:470ux2cux22typeux22:ux22webpageux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Globalux20EVux20Outlookux202024ux20-ux20Analysisux20andux20keyux20findings.ux20Aux20reportux20byux20theux20Internationalux20Energyux20Agency.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22IEAux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22en-GBux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Trendsux20inux20electricux20carsux20ux2013ux20Globalux20EVux20Outlookux202024ux20ux2013ux20Analysisux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.iea.orgux2freportsux2fglobal-ev-outlook-2024ux2ftrends-in-electric-carsux22ux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c10ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDKD8nMSFBCn}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-13}{}{}The
transition toward the sustainable transportation has increased the need
of understanding the consumer opinion towards electric cars. This study
aims at applying the sentiment analysis to identify the gaps that needs
to be bridged for adoption of electric cars. We will analyze the
user-generated content from three countries---Germany, China, and
Norway---to gain insight into the public sentiment related to electric
cars and behavioral trends with regards to them. The chosen regions
present significant contrast when it comes to adoption rates of electric
cars. Norway remains on top with the highest market penetration, with
about 95\% of the new car sales already done in 2023. In China, electric
car adoption had soared high, more than 40\% of the market share by
early 2024. Germany had witnessed ups and downs, with electric cars
making 30\% in 2022 {[}27{]}.

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-14}{}{}The recent breakthroughs in natural
language processing (NLP), especially the emergence of transformer-based
deep learning models, have changed the panorama of sentiment analysis.
Different from classical methods relying on manual construction of
dictionaries or certain statistical models, these advanced techniques
can interpret contextual meaning, detect subtle linguistic nuances, and
process user-generated content at a much larger scale

a much larger scale efficiently{[}28{]}. According to research, social
media conversation and online discussion forums sentiment analysis
affects market trends and consumer behaviors

ux20wasux20conductedux20toux20explainux20theux20willingnessux20toux20buyux20anux20electricux20carux20byux20usingux20theux20informationux20ofux201245ux20Spanishux20respondents.ux20Ourux20resultsux20indicateux20thatux20theux20higherux20aux20consumerux27sux20perceptionux20ofux20theux20priceux20ofux20electricux20vehiclesux20andux20theux20longerux20chargingux20timesux20areux2cux20theux20lessux20aux20consumerux27sux20willingnessux20toux20buyux20aux20newux20electricux20carux20is.ux20Theux20resultsux20areux20usefulux20toux20defineux20keyux20elementsux20linkedux20toux20theux20mostux20appropriateux20industrialux20policyux20whichux20helpsux20companiesux20promoteux20theux20electricux20vehicle.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Technologicalux20Forecastingux20andux20Socialux20Changeux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.techfore.2016.05.006ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux220040-1625ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22Technologicalux20Forecastingux20andux20Socialux20Changeux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux226-14ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22ScienceDirectux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Analyzingux20consumerux20attitudesux20towardsux20electricux20vehicleux20purchasingux20intentionsux20inux20Spain:ux20Technologicalux20limitationsux20andux20vehicleux20confidenceux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Analyzingux20consumerux20attitudesux20towardsux20electricux20vehicleux20purchasingux20intentionsux20inux20Spainux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.sciencedirect.comux2fscienceux2farticleux2fpiiux2fS0040162516300658ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux22109ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Junqueraux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Beatrizux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Morenoux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Blancaux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22uxc1lvarezux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Robertoux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c10ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222016ux22ux2c8ux2c1ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDI3ZhBKhpZY}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-14}{}{}The
recent breakthroughs in natural language processing (NLP), especially
the emergence of transformer-based deep learning models, have changed
the panorama of sentiment analysis. Different from classical methods
relying on manual construction of dictionaries or certain statistical
models, these advanced techniques can interpret contextual meaning,
detect subtle linguistic nuances, and process user-generated content at
a much larger scale efficiently{[}28{]}. According to research, social
media conversation and online discussion forums sentiment analysis
affects market trends and consumer behaviors appreciably{[}29{]}. By
applying the latest NLP models, this study will provide more insights
into the differences in how people perceive electric cars.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-15}{}{}Research Question
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-16}{}{}With environmental awareness and
government incentives, the uptake of electric vehicles has been slower
than expected. One major challenge lies in understanding the reasons for
consumer aspects of perceptions and their impacts on purchasing
decisions. This study will explore:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  How do user generated content impact on electric cars sentiments?
\item
  What are the primary concerns and motivations driving EC adoption or
  resistance?
\item
  Does sentiment analysis able to predict the future of electric car?
\item
  What are different factors impacting the difference of electric car
  adoption in Germany, China and Norway?
\end{enumerate}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-17}{}{}Objectives
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-18}{}{}The primary objective of this
research is to analyze consumer sentiment toward electric cars (ECs)
using sentiment analysis techniques. This study aims to provide insights
into the factors influencing EC adoption in Germany, China, and Norway
by implementing natural language processing (NLP) models. The specific
objectives of the thesis are:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-19}{}{}Perform sentiment analysis on the
  user generated content present at various online platforms. 
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-20}{}{}Identify the barriers and drivers
  for electric car adoption.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-21}{}{}Evaluate the application of NLP
  models for electric car sentiments
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-22}{}{}Compare consumer perception in
  three different regions Norway, Germany and China. 
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-23}{}{}Literature Review
\end{enumerate}

The adoption of electric cars (ECs) has generated public discussions on
a mass scale, with consumers enthusiastically sharing their opinions
over social media, online forums, product reviews, and surveys.
Sentiment analysis or opinion mining has emerged as a widely used tool
to examine such discourse, gaining insights from consumer-created text
to measure the trends in public opinion. Earlier studies have employed
sentiment analysis in many fields, including marketing, product
adoption, and policy assessment

esux20isux20limitedux20andux20theux20artificialux20irrationalux20behaviorux20willux20affectux20theux20correctnessux20ofux20theux20resultsux2cux20soux20theux20traditionalux20methodux20isux20notux20universal.ux20Withux20theux20developmentux20ofux20deepux20learningux2cux20textux20pre-trainingux20languageux20modelux20andux20knowledgeux20graphux20technologyux20continueux20toux20develop.ux20Aimingux20atux20theux20researchux20ofux20sentimentux20analysisux20methodsux20forux20textux20dataux2cux20weux20summarizeux20theux20researchux20backgroundux20andux20domesticux20andux20foreignux20researchux20statusux20ofux20sentimentux20analysisux20methodsux20forux20textux20dataux2cux20andux20exploreux20theux20hotux20researchux20contentux2cux20keyux20problemsux2cux20commonlyux20usedux20experimentalux20methodsux20andux20technicalux20linesux20ofux20sentimentux20analysisux20methodsux20forux20textux20textux20data.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Frontiersux20inux20Computingux20andux20Intelligentux20Systemsux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.54097ux2ffcis.v3i1.6022ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux222832-6024ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux221ux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22licenseux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fcreativecommons.orgux2flicensesux2fbyux2f4.0ux22ux2cux22noteux22:ux22number:ux201ux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux2242-47ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22drpress.orgux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Researchux20onux20sentimentux20analysisux20methodsux20forux20text-orientedux20dataux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fdrpress.orgux2fojsux2findex.phpux2ffcisux2farticleux2fviewux2f6022ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux223ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Dengux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Yuanfeiux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c21ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222023ux22ux2c3ux2c17ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDZZqsijnT6P}{}{}The
adoption of electric cars (ECs) has generated public discussions on a
mass scale, with consumers enthusiastically sharing their opinions over
social media, online forums, product reviews, and surveys. Sentiment
analysis or opinion mining has emerged as a widely used tool to examine
such discourse, gaining insights from consumer-created text to measure
the trends in public opinion. Earlier studies have employed sentiment
analysis in many fields, including marketing, product adoption, and
policy assessment {[}30{]}. In the case of EC adoption, sentiment
analysis has been applied to examine consumers' sentiments about various
factors such as battery efficiency, charging stations, and incentives
offered by the government
