Figure1: CRISP-DM Model

The CRISP-DM (Cross-Industry Standard Process for Data Mining) model
provides a structured framework for analyzing sentiment data and
predicting electric cars adoption trends. By integrating sentiment
analysis, survey generation, and predictive modeling, this approach
ensures a systematic, data-driven methodology for understanding public
perception across different regions
\protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22HrOfS3mDux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d40ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d40ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:516ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fHBZGNRDXux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:516ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Thisux20researchux20employsux20theux20CRISP-DMux20frameworkux20toux20analyzeux20sentimentux20andux20toxicityux20dynamicsux20inux20touristux20vlogux20reviewsux20thoroughly.ux20Theux20studyux20delvesux20intoux20sentimentux20classificationux20andux20toxicityux20identificationux20nuancesux20byux20leveragingux20machineux20learningux20algorithmsux20suchux20asux20k-NNux2cux20SVMux2cux20NBCux2cux20andux20DTux20withux20SMOTE.ux20Utilizingux20aux20datasetux20comprisingux20aux20substantialux20numberux20ofux20postsux2cux20theux20analysisux20revealsux20varyingux20levelsux20ofux20accuracyux20acrossux20differentux20algorithms.ux20Forux20instanceux2cux20k-NNux20andux20SVMux20showcaseux20promisingux20accuracyux20ratesux20ofux2085.90ux5cux25ux20andux2086.27ux5cux25ux20inux20sentimentux20classificationux2cux20whileux20NBCux20andux20DTux20withux20SMOTEux20yieldux2072.52ux5cux25ux20andux2071.14ux5cux25ux2cux20respectively.ux20Furthermoreux2cux20theux20researchux20elucidatesux20theux20limitationsux20ofux20toxicityux20analysisux2cux20withux20NBCux20demonstratingux20aux20precisionux20ofux2064.96ux5cux25ux20andux20DTux20exhibitingux20lowerux20recallux20rates.ux20Theseux20findingsux20highlightux20theux20importanceux20ofux20robustux20methodologiesux20forux20understandingux20sentimentux20andux20toxicityux20dynamicsux20inux20onlineux20contentux2cux20particularlyux20inux20touristux20vlogux20reviews.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Journalux20ofux20Computerux20Systemux20andux20Informaticsux20ux28JoSYCux29ux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.47065ux2fjosyc.v5i3.5154ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux222714-8912ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux223ux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22licenseux22:ux22Copyrightux20ux28cux29ux202024ux20Yerikux20Afriantoux20Singgalenux22ux2cux22noteux22:ux22number:ux203ux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux22648-659ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22ejurnal.seminar-id.comux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Comprehensiveux20Analysisux20ofux20Sentimentux20andux20Toxicityux20Dynamicsux20inux20Touristux20Vlogux20Reviews:ux20Aux20CRISP-DMux20Approachux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Comprehensiveux20Analysisux20ofux20Sentimentux20andux20Toxicityux20Dynamicsux20inux20Touristux20Vlogux20Reviewsux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fejurnal.seminar-id.comux2findex.phpux2fjosycux2farticleux2fviewux2f5154ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux225ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Singgalenux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Yerikux20Afriantoux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c21ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222024ux22ux2c5ux2c31ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDMYSim2iRp9}{}{}The
CRISP-DM (Cross-Industry Standard Process for Data Mining) model
provides a structured framework for analyzing sentiment data and
predicting electric cars adoption trends. By integrating sentiment
analysis, survey generation, and predictive modeling, this approach
ensures a systematic, data-driven methodology for understanding public
perception across different regions {[}40{]}.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-82}{}{}Business Understanding
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

The objective is to analyze sentiment data to identify key factors
influencing e cars adoption in Norway, China, and Germany. Challenges
include regional variations, multilingual sentiment analysis, and biases
in user-generated content (UGC). To address these challenges, the
approach integrates trusted content-based surveys, sentiment dictionary
creation, and machine learning models for enhanced real-time sentiment
tracking.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-83}{}{}Data Understanding
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Data is collected from multiple sources, including social media, forums,
product reviews, surveys, and online articles. Exploratory data analysis
techniques such as word clouds, frequency charts, and pie charts help
identify dominant sentiment trends. Additionally, cosine similarity is
used to compare sentiment distributions across different regions,
enabling a better understanding of country-specific perceptions of EC
adoption.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-84}{}{}Data Preparation
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Raw text data undergoes preprocessing to improve analysis accuracy. This
includes tokenization, stopword removal, stemming, and lemmatization.
Sentiment classification is performed using a lexicon-based approach,
where words are categorized as positive, negative, or neutral based on a
predefined sentiment dictionary. Additionally, survey questions are
generated using T5-based Transformer models, ensuring structured data
collection from users.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-85}{}{}Modeling
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Various machine learning models are applied to predict future e-cars
adoption trends based on sentiment scores. Regression models such as
linear regression, KNN, and random forest establish relationships
between sentiment trends and adoption rates. LSTM networks help identify
sequential sentiment patterns over time, while K-means clustering groups
similar sentiment trends across different regions, aiding in
understanding consumer preferences and concerns.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-86}{}{}Evaluation
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Model performance is assessed using standard evaluation metrics.
Sentiment predictions are validated against survey responses to ensure
accuracy. Additionally, silhouette scores for clustering and cosine
similarity for regional comparisons provide insights into how well
sentiment patterns align across different markets. The results help
policymakers and manufacturers fine-tune their strategies based on
consumer sentiment trends.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-87}{}{}Deployment
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\emph{}

The final models and insights are deployed in real-time sentiment
analysis dashboards, providing stakeholders with data-driven
decision-making tools. The key applications include tracking public
sentiment trends toward e-cars across regions, identifying major
consumer concerns and adoption barriers, and enhancing marketing
strategies and infrastructure planning based on sentiment insights.

\emph{}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-88}{}{}Challenges
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-89}{}{}Identified Problems for e-cars
      Sentiment Analysis
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Regional Comparison Limitation}}} -- Limited
  sentiment analysis comparing \textbf{\textbf{Norway, China, and
  Germany}}, making it difficult to understand country-specific adoption
  trends.
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Multilingual Sentiment Challenges}}} -- Lack of
  robust sentiment analysis models for \textbf{\textbf{non-English
  user-generated content (UGC)}} in \textbf{\textbf{Chinese, German, and
  Norwegian}}.
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Bias in Sentiment Sources}}}\emph{ }-- Heavy
  reliance on \textbf{\textbf{online discussions and reviews}} may
  introduce bias, affecting the accuracy of sentiment evaluation.
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{R}}\emph{\textbf{eal-time sentiment analysis}}}
  -- Existing \textbf{\textbf{sentiment tracking models}} require
  improvements for \textbf{\textbf{real-time sentiment analysis}},
  enhancing accuracy and responsiveness.
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-90}{}{}Proposed Solutions
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\section{}\label{section}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Survey-Based Sentiment Validation}}} -- Generate
  surveys from \textbf{\textbf{trusted content sources}} to reduce bias
  and obtain structured user feedback on electric cars adoption.
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Sentiment Dictionary Development}}} -- Create a
  \textbf{\textbf{domain-specific lexicon}} for
  \textbf{\textbf{positive}\textbf{, }\textbf{negative}\textbf{,
  }\textbf{and neutral words}}, improving sentiment classification
  across multiple languages.
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Matrix-Based Sentiment Analysis}}} -- Use
  \textbf{\textbf{mathematical matrix methods}} to classify and analyze
  survey responses for more structured sentiment evaluation.
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Automated Question Generation}}}\emph{ }--
  Implement a \textbf{\textbf{Question Generation Model}} to create
  \textbf{\textbf{Likert-scale survey questions}}, ensuring consistency
  and relevance.
\item
  \textbf{\emph{\textbf{Context Summarization Using Transformers}}} --
  Apply \textbf{\textbf{Transformer models}} to summarize large text
  datasets, improving sentiment extraction and survey generation
  accuracy.
\end{itemize}

By implementing these solutions, e-cars\textbf{\textbf{ sentiment
analysis can be made more structured, multilingual, and data-driven}},
allowing for better insights into \textbf{\textbf{regional adoption
patterns}}.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-91}{}{}Data 
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-92}{}{}Understanding the opinions of people
regarding electric cars (ECs) requires a systematic and disparate
dataset, merging social media comment, product review, news bulletin,
and questionnaire. However, EC sentiment analysis is subject to several
issues such as geographical variability, multilinguality, and source
prejudice. Herein, the data processing and collection phase, overview of
main issues, and proposed remedies from evidence-based suggestions are
offered in order to promote enhanced precision and objectivity in
sentiment analysis.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-93}{}{}Collection
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-94}{}{}Data for EC sentiment analysis are
collected from several sources to include a range of opinions. The
primary sources of data are:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-95}{}{}Social Media Platforms (Twitter):
  Social media conveys live opinions, debates, and conversations on EC
  adoption, infrastructure, and experience. Studies have found that
  sentiment analysis based on Twitter can effectively track trends in
  consumer attitudes

futureux20policiesux20aimedux20atux20promotingux20theux20adoptionux20ofux20electricux20vehicles.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Sustainabilityux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.3390ux2fsu16010305ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux222071-1050ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux221ux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22licenseux22:ux22http:ux2fux2fcreativecommons.orgux2flicensesux2fbyux2f3.0ux2fux22ux2cux22noteux22:ux22number:ux201ux2fnpublisher:ux20Multidisciplinaryux20Digitalux20Publishingux20Instituteux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux22305ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22www.mdpi.comux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Demographic-Basedux20Publicux20Perceptionux20Analysisux20ofux20Electricux20Vehiclesux20onux20Onlineux20Socialux20Networksux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.mdpi.comux2f2071-1050ux2f16ux2f1ux2f305ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux2216ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Priyamux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Tavishiux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Ruanux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Taoux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Lvux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Qinux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c9ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222024ux22ux2c1ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDtmVHkwzM5G}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-95}{}{}Social
  Media Platforms (Twitter): Social media conveys live opinions,
  debates, and conversations on EC adoption, infrastructure, and
  experience. Studies have found that sentiment analysis based on
  Twitter can effectively track trends in consumer attitudes {[}8{]}.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-96}{}{}Consumer Reviews (Yelp): Product
  and consumer reviews help to make inferences of user satisfaction and
  problems. Research shows that sentiment analysis of reviews provides
  actionable feedback on the public's opinion about EC performance and
  features
  \protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22cAncQgedux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d51ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d51ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:532ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fR3YICXXXux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:532ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Appl.ux20Softux20Comput.ux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.asoc.2023.110745ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux221568-4946ux22ux2cux22issueux22:ux22Cux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22ACMux20Digitalux20Libraryux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Sentimentux20analysisux20ofux20onlineux20reviewsux20forux20electricux20vehiclesux20usingux20theux20SMAA-2ux20methodux20andux20intervalux20type-2ux20fuzzyux20setsux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fdoi.orgux2f10.1016ux2fj.asoc.2023.110745ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux22147ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Gongux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Bengangux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Liuux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ruiux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Zhangux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Xiaoqiux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Changux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ching-Terux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Liuux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Zhiux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c21ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222023ux22ux2c11ux2c1ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDkRrtnJXlQW}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-96}{}{}Consumer
  Reviews (Yelp): Product and consumer reviews help to make inferences
  of user satisfaction and problems. Research shows that sentiment
  analysis of reviews provides actionable feedback on the public's
  opinion about EC performance and features {[}51{]} 
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-97}{}{}News Articles and Blogs: News
  sources supply data regarding government policy, market trends, and
  new technological innovations in ECs. Research suggests that media
  framing influences EC adoption rates .
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-98}{}{}Surveys and Questionnaires: Surveys
  aid researchers in acquiring focused views regarding EC adoption
  drivers such as cost, range anxiety, and the environment
  \protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22Wajikc00ux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d52ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d52ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:533ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fKJVWGYKLux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:533ux2cux22typeux22:ux22article-journalux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Canux20changesux20inux20consumerux20psychologyux20inducedux20byux20mediaux20attentionux20leadux20toux20pro-environmentalux20transportationux20behaviorsux20orux20choicesux3fux20Inux20responseux2cux20weux20matchux20aux20uniqueux20datasetux20onux20mediaux20attentionux20withux20electricux20vehicleux20ux28EVux29ux20salesux20dataux20inux20Chinaux2cux20employingux20aux20two-wayux20fixedux20effectsux20modelux20andux20anux20instrumentalux20variableux20ux28IVux29ux20methodux20toux20estimateux20theux20impactux20ofux20mediaux20attentionux20onux20EVux20adoption.ux20Ourux20researchux20foundux20thatux20mediaux20attentionux20significantlyux20acceleratesux20theux20marketux20diffusionux20ofux20EVs.ux20Thisux20resultux20isux20substantiatedux20throughux20IVux20testsux20andux20aux20seriesux20ofux20robustnessux20checks.ux20Additionallyux2cux20theux20mediationux20effectux20modelux20revealedux20thatux20mediaux20attentionux20indirectlyux20influencesux20EVux20adoptionux20throughux20consumerux20EVux20attentionux2cux20governmentux20EVux20focusux2cux20EVux20technologicalux20innovationux2cux20andux20EVux20purchaseux20subsidies.ux20Weux20alsoux20discoveredux20thatux20theux20effectux20ofux20mediaux20attentionux20onux20EVux20adoptionux20isux20notux20onlyux20positivelyux20moderatedux20byux20theux20levelux20ofux20consumerux20policyux20awarenessux20andux20educationux20butux20isux20alsoux20moreux20pronouncedux20inux20areasux20withux20highux20populationux20densityux2cux20highux20incomeux2cux20highux20educationux20levelsux2cux20andux20aux20highux20airux20qualityux20index.ux20Ourux20findingsux20notux20onlyux20provideux20empiricalux20evidenceux20forux20understandingux20theux20roleux20ofux20socialux20psychologicalux20factorsux20andux20externalux20mediaux20stimuliux20inux20promotingux20behavioralux20changesux20towardsux20environmentalux20protectionux20butux20alsoux20offerux20guidanceux20forux20policymakersux20andux20businessesux20inux20implementingux20effectiveux20marketux20promotionux20strategiesux20andux20formulatingux20relevantux20policiesux2cux20especiallyux20inux20enhancingux20publicux20cognitionux20andux20acceptanceux20ofux20EVs.ux22ux2cux22container-titleux22:ux22Transportationux20Researchux20Partux20A:ux20Policyux20andux20Practiceux22ux2cux22DOIux22:ux2210.1016ux2fj.tra.2024.104269ux22ux2cux22ISSNux22:ux220965-8564ux22ux2cux22journalAbbreviationux22:ux22Transportationux20Researchux20Partux20A:ux20Policyux20andux20Practiceux22ux2cux22pageux22:ux22104269ux22ux2cux22sourceux22:ux22ScienceDirectux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Mediaux20attentionux20andux20electricux20vehicleux20adoption:ux20Evidenceux20fromux20275ux20citiesux20inux20Chinaux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Mediaux20attentionux20andux20electricux20vehicleux20adoptionux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fwww.sciencedirect.comux2fscienceux2farticleux2fpiiux2fS0965856424003173ux22ux2cux22volumeux22:ux22190ux22ux2cux22authorux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Zhaoux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Xiaoleiux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Liux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Xuemeiux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Zhaoux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Ziyaoux22ux5cux7dux2cux5cux7bux22familyux22:ux22Luoux22ux2cux22givenux22:ux22Tianyiux22ux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c21ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux2cux22issuedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222024ux22ux2c12ux2c1ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDDOLsv7u2aC}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-98}{}{}Surveys
  and Questionnaires: Surveys aid researchers in acquiring focused views
  regarding EC adoption drivers such as cost, range anxiety, and the
  environment {[}52{]}.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-99}{}{}Government and Industry Reports:
  Organizational reports by agencies like the International Energy
  Agency (IEA) and BloombergNEF provide statistical data on EC sales,
  the growth of infrastructure, and policy impacts.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-100}{}{}It gathers information using web
scraping techniques (BeautifulSoup, Scrapy) and API calls (Twitter API,
Reddit API) to facilitate continuous monitoring of public sentiment.

\emph{}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-101}{}{}\emph{Challenge}:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\roman{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-102}{}{} Lot of the online resources are
  paid
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-103}{}{}Scrapping of data from websites
  are prohibited 
\end{enumerate}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-104}{}{}Preprocessing
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-105}{}{}Before sentiment analysis is done,
data collected is preprocessed to remove inconsistencies and increase
model accuracy. The following steps are carried out:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-106}{}{}Text Tokenization: Splitting text
  into individual words or phrases for processing.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-107}{}{}Stopword Removal: Extraction of
  common words (e.g., "the," "and") that have no impact on sentiment
  detection.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-108}{}{}Lemmatization \& Stemming:
  Bringing words down to their root word (e.g., "driving" → "drive") to
  standardize vocabulary.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-109}{}{}Noise Removal: Filtering out
  special characters, emojis, and hyperlinks that do not add value to
  sentiment classification.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-110}{}{}Language Detection \& Translation:
  Since EC discussions occur in multiple languages, automated
  translation models (Google's T5, BERT, NLTK) are used to standardize
  data across English, Chinese, German, and Norwegian sources
  \protect\hypertarget{ZOTERO_ITEMux20CSL_CITATIONux20ux5cux7bux22citationIDux22:ux22vwLDLlIWux22ux2cux22propertiesux22:ux5cux7bux22formattedCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d53ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22plainCitationux22:ux22ux7bux5bux7d53ux7bux5dux7dux22ux2cux22noteIndexux22:0ux5cux7dux2cux22citationItemsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux5cux7bux22idux22:537ux2cux22urisux22:ux7bux5bux7dux22http:ux2fux2fzotero.orgux2fusersux2f9887326ux2fitemsux2fUZ2GF5DVux22ux7bux5dux7dux2cux22itemDataux22:ux5cux7bux22idux22:537ux2cux22typeux22:ux22webpageux22ux2cux22abstractux22:ux22Postedux20byux20Adamux20Robertsux2cux20Staffux20Softwareux20Engineerux20andux20Colinux20Raffelux2cux20Seniorux20Researchux20Scientistux2cux20Googleux20Researchux20ux20ux20Overux20theux20pastux20fewux20yearsux2cux20transferux20le...ux22ux2cux22languageux22:ux22enux22ux2cux22titleux22:ux22Exploringux20Transferux20Learningux20withux20T5:ux20theux20Text-To-Textux20Transferux20Transformerux22ux2cux22title-shortux22:ux22Exploringux20Transferux20Learningux20withux20T5ux22ux2cux22URLux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fresearch.googleux2fblogux2fexploring-transfer-learning-with-t5-the-text-to-text-transfer-transformerux2fux22ux2cux22accessedux22:ux5cux7bux22date-partsux22:ux7bux5bux7dux7bux5bux7dux222025ux22ux2c2ux2c21ux7bux5dux7dux7bux5dux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux5cux7dux7bux5dux7dux2cux22schemaux22:ux22https:ux2fux2fgithub.comux2fcitation-style-languageux2fschemaux2frawux2fmasterux2fcsl-citation.jsonux22ux5cux7dux20RNDgGCuW3XcgR}{}{}\protect\hypertarget{anchor-110}{}{}Language
  Detection \& Translation: Since EC discussions occur in multiple
  languages, automated translation models (Google's T5, BERT, NLTK) are
  used to standardize data across English, Chinese, German, and
  Norwegian sources {[}53{]}.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-111}{}{}By applying these pre-processing
techniques, the dataset becomes cleaner and more structured, leading to
improved sentiment classification accuracy.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-112}{}{}Biasing
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-113}{}{}Data bias may skew sentiment
analysis results. Common biases include:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-114}{}{}Platform Bias: Social media may
  overrepresent specific groups (e.g., tech-savvy individuals on Yelp).
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-115}{}{}Brand Bias: Favorable or
  unfavorable attitudes towards particular EC brands based on
  advertising influence.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-116}{}{}Geographic Bias: Public opinion
  varies from place to place due to differences in infrastructure and
  policies.
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-117}{}{}Bias Mitigation Strategies
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-118}{}{}As a measure to eliminate bias in
sentiment analysis, survey usage from trustworthy sources is a common
practice that boosts data integrity and gives an improved picture of the
people's opinions.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-119}{}{}\emph{Triangulation}: Utilizing
  various data sources or methods for cross-validation makes the result
  more credible.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-120}{}{}\emph{Blinding}: Not informing
  participants about specific treatment can prevent response and
  interpretation bias.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-121}{}{}\emph{Neutral Question Design
  (Like-rt Scale)}: Framing questions that are free from biased language
  and do not suggest a desired response helps in obtaining genuine
  responses.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-122}{}{}\emph{Ensuring Anonymity and
  Confidentiality}: Assuring respondents that their identity and answers
  will be kept confidential encourages them to respond truthfully and
  reduces social desirability bias.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-123}{}{}By implementing these methods,
researchers are able to increase the accuracy of sentiment analysis and
better understand people's true sentiments.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-124}{}{}Survey Generation
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-125}{}{}Surveys are intended to capture
people's sentiment towards EC adoption. The construction of a survey
entails the following:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-126}{}{}Key Sentiment Dimensions: Focus
  revolve around considerations like prices, charging concern, charging
  infrastructure, and subsidy by the government.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-127}{}{}Application of AI-Based Models:
  Transformer models like T5 generate fair survey questions.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-128}{}{}Survey Distribution Across
  Populations: Encouraging diverse user groups to participate maximizes
  representativeness.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-129}{}{}Data Adaptation for Sentiment
  Analysis: Collected responses are transformed into sentiment-labeled
  datasets (positive, neutral, negative) to train models.
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-130}{}{}Methodology
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-131}{}{}This research study employs a
scientific approach of electric car sentiment analysis and adoption
pattern prediction using machine learning. The approach integrates data
collection, sentiment analysis, questionnaire design, machine learning
prediction, and regional sentiments comparison, as stipulated by the
CRISP-DM (Cross-Industry Standard Process for Data Mining) framework.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-132}{}{}\textbf{ CRISP-DM Framework for
    Sentiment Analysis}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-133}{}{}The CRISP-DM cycle consists of six
phases:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-134}{}{}\emph{Business Understanding}: The
  primary objective is to assess public sentiment toward electric
  vehicles and predict adoption trends. Understanding public opinion is
  crucial for stakeholders, including manufacturers and policymakers, to
  make informed decision.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-135}{}{}\emph{Understanding Data:}
  Extracting and probing data from disparate sources, like web forums,
  questionnaires, and market tendencies.
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-136}{}{}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-137}{}{}File Mode: Reading data from
      existing files.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-138}{}{}URL Mode: Web scraping
      techniques to extract data from websites.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-139}{}{}Survey Mode: Collecting
      responses from distributed surveys.
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-140}{}{}\emph{Preparation of Data}: Text
  data pre-processing and preparing sentiment analysis and forecast
  datasets.
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-141}{}{}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-142}{}{}Tokenization: Splitting text
      into individual words or tokens.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-143}{}{}Stopword Removal: Eliminating
      common words that do not contribute to sentiment.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-144}{}{}Stemming and Lemmatization:
      Reducing words to their root forms to ensure uniformity.
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-145}{}{}\emph{Modeling}: 
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-146}{}{}Two primary approaches are utilized
for sentiment analysis:

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-147}{}{}Lexicon-Based Analysis: This
      method assigns predefined sentiment scores to words and calculates
      the overall sentiment of a document based on these scores.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-148}{}{}Transformer-Based Analysis:
      Leveraging models like BERT, this approach captures contextual
      nuances in text, providing a more sophisticated sentiment
      classification 
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-149}{}{}For forecasting EV adoption, various
machine learning models are employed:

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-150}{}{}Linear Regression: Establishes
      a relationship between sentiment scores and EV adoption rates.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-151}{}{}K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN):
      Predicts adoption based on similarities in sentiment data.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-152}{}{}Random Forest: Utilizes
      ensemble learning to enhance prediction accuracy.
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-153}{}{}Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM):
      Captures temporal dependencies in time-series data to forecast
      future trends.
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-154}{}{}\emph{Evaluating}: Measuring the
  performance of the model, the accuracy of the sentiment, and the
  effectiveness of forecasting. The models are compared in terms of
  metrics such as accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score.
  Cross-validation techniques are used to ensure robustness of the
  models. Visualizations such as pie charts, word clouds, and bar charts
  are utilized to display distributions of sentiment and most
  significant findings in an effective way.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-155}{}{}\emph{Deployment}: Visualization
  of results in terms of sentiment distribution, geographical
  variations, and adoption trends.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-156}{}{}\textbf{Sentiment Analysis and
    Electric Car Adoption Algorithm}

    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-157}{}{} Data Gathering
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-158}{}{}The data gathering process is to
gather diverse datasets for sentiment analysis and forecasting. The
algorithm supports three modes of data gathering:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-159}{}{}File-based Data: Retrieving text
  data from existing documents.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-160}{}{}Web Scraping: Retrieving
  sentiment-based discussions from web sources.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-161}{}{}Survey-Based Data: Designing
  organized survey responses to minimize bias.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-162}{}{}After collection, text preprocessing
operations such as tokenization, removal of stopwords, stemming, and
lemmatization are employed to prepare data for sentiment analysis.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-163}{}{}Sentiment Analysis
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-164}{}{}Lexicon-Based Sentiment Analysis:
  Employs sentiment dictionaries to score text sentiment on
  pre-determined positive and negative words.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-165}{}{}Transformer-Based Sentiment
  Analysis: Employs deep learning models (e.g., BERT) to classify based
  on contextual embeddings.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-166}{}{}Text content is vectorized using
TF-IDF (Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency) or word embeddings,
and sentiment classification comes next.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-167}{}{}Survey Question Generation
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-168}{}{}A survey question generation module
is employed as part of sentiment analysis augmentation. This is done to
impose structured user feedback through:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-169}{}{}T5 Transformer Model: Generates
  questions programmatically from abstractive content.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-170}{}{}Feature Extraction from Surveys:
  Extracts salient features in order to maintain relevance.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-171}{}{}The surveys enhance sentiment
verification and reduce biases due to online sources.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-172}{}{}Machine Learning Predictions
      on Adoption of Electric Cars
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-173}{}{}In predicting electric car adoption,
data of sentiment is combined with sales data. Numerous machine learning
models are utilized:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-174}{}{}Linear Regression: Tracks linear
  relationships between sentiment scores and adoption trends.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-175}{}{}K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN):
  Predicts future adoption patterns from similar historical data.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-176}{}{}Random Forest: Aggregates numerous
  decision trees to produce higher accuracy.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-177}{}{}Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM):
  Tracks sequential patterns in sentiment and sales data over time.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-178}{}{}The model predicts future adoption
rates from historical sentiment and sales trends.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-179}{}{}Sentiment Visualization 
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-180}{}{}Visualization techniques are
employed to effectively interpret results:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-181}{}{}Pie Charts: Display sentiment
  distribution.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-182}{}{}Word Clouds: Highlight frequent
  words in sentiment data.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-183}{}{}Bar Charts: Show sentiment
  frequency patterns.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-184}{}{}For regional sentiment analysis,
  vector similarity techniques (e.g., Cosine Similarity) compare
  sentiment trends across countries.
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \protect\hypertarget{anchor-185}{}{}Execution Flow
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-186}{}{}The method is organized in a
workflow:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-187}{}{}Sentiment Analysis Implementation:
  Preprocessing, sentiment classification, data collection, and
  visualization.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-188}{}{}Survey Processing: Survey question
  generation and response analysis.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-189}{}{}Electric Car Adoption Prediction:
  Predictive model training and future trend forecasting.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-190}{}{}Final Deployment: Reporting
  insights through dashboards and comparison analysis.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-191}{}{}Through this CRISP-DM step-by-step
method, sentiment analysis is structured, data-driven, and optimized for
electric car adoption prediction.

1: procedure FineTuneBERT()

2: Dataset ← LoadEVSentimentDataset()

TokenizedData ← TokenizeAndEncode(Dataset)

Model ← LoadPreTrainedBERT()

FineTunedModel ← TrainModel(Model, TokenizedData)

SaveModel(FineTunedModel)

3: return FineTunedModel

4: end procedure

5: procedure EvaluateModel()

6: FineTunedModel ← LoadFineTunedBERT()

TestData ← LoadTestDataset()

Predictions ← GetPredictions(FineTunedModel, TestData)

Metrics ← ComputeEvaluationMetrics(Predictions, TestData)

7: return Metrics

8: end procedure

9: procedure RunSentimentAnalysis()

10: FineTunedModel ← FineTuneBERT()

SentimentScores ← EvaluateModel(FineTunedModel)

PlotSentimentResults(SentimentScores)

11: return SentimentScores

12: end procedure

13: procedure Main()

14: RunSentimentAnalysis()

15: end procedure

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-292}{}{}\emph{Figure8: Sentiment
Distribution (English)}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-293}{}{} Positive Sentiments

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-294}{}{}Environmental Benefits: Frequent
  terms like renewable, clean, environment, and sustainability highlight
  optimism about EVs reducing fossil fuel dependency and mitigating
  climate change.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-295}{}{}Technological Advancements: Words
  such as efficient, electrified, performance, and fast emphasize
  perceived improvements in EV efficiency, speed, and driving
  experience.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-296}{}{}Cost-Effectiveness: Terms like
  cost-effective and emissions-free suggest recognition of long-term
  economic and environmental savings.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-297}{}{}The dominance of positive sentiment
underscores public confidence in EVs as a sustainable and innovative
solution.

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-298}{}{}Negative Sentiments

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-299}{}{}Infrastructure Limitations:
  Repetitive terms like charging, infrastructure, range, and slow
  reflect anxiety about inadequate charging networks and range anxiety.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-300}{}{}Battery Issues: Frequent mentions
  of battery (e.g., lifespan, reliability) and maintenance indicate
  technical concerns.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-301}{}{}Cost Barriers: Words like
  expensive, costly, and limited point to affordability challenges and
  perceived high upfront costs.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-302}{}{}Implications:

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-303}{}{}Addressing charging infrastructure
gaps, improving battery technology transparency, and offering subsidies
could alleviate these concerns. Regional disparities (as seen in cosine
similarity results) suggest localized infrastructure investments may be
necessary.

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-304}{}{}Neutral Sentiments

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-305}{}{}Technical Specifications: Terms
  like technology, hybrid, motor, powertrain, and fast-charging
  dominate, reflecting factual discussions about EV components and
  innovations.
\item
  \protect\hypertarget{anchor-306}{}{}Industry Terminology: Words such
  as plug-in, charger, and battery-electric indicate neutral debates on
  EV types and technical features.
\end{itemize}

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-307}{}{}Implications:

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-308}{}{}Neutral sentiments often represent
educational or informational content. This suggests opportunities for
stakeholders to provide clearer technical communication to bridge
knowledge gaps and convert neutral discussions into positive sentiment.

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-333}{}{}Positive Sentiments

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-334}{}{}Electric vehicles are widely praised
for their sustainability (bærekraftig), eco-friendliness (miljøvennlig),
and efficiency (effektiv). Users highlight benefits like fast charging
(hurtiglading), modern design (moderne), and a quiet ride (stille).
Terms such as smart (smart) and zero emissions (nullutslipp) reflect
enthusiasm for advanced, climate-friendly technology.

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-335}{}{}Negative Sentiments

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-336}{}{}Key concerns include battery life
(batteri), range anxiety (rekkeviddeangst), and charging infrastructure
(ladeinfrastruktur). Words like expensive (dyrt), slow charging (langsom
lading), and maintenance costs (vedlikeholdskostnader) highlight
affordability and practicality issues, making EV adoption challenging.

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-337}{}{}Neutral Sentiments

\protect\hypertarget{anchor-338}{}{}Neutral discussions focus on
technical aspects, including electric vehicles (elbil), chargers
(lader), battery capacity (batterikapasitet), and energy systems
(energisystem). Mentions of hybrid (hybrid) and car industry
(bilindustri) indicate broader industry conversations.

The \textbf{\textbf{CRISP-DM (Cross-Industry Standard Process for Data
Mining)}} methodology is a structured approach to data mining and
machine learning projects. It consists of \textbf{\textbf{six phases}},
which were followed in our \textbf{\textbf{Transformer-based sentiment
analysis}} for electric cars.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Business Understanding
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Using a \textbf{\textbf{Transformer-based model (BERT)}} to classify
sentiment into \textbf{\textbf{positive, neutral, and negative}}
categories.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Data Understanding
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}

  Sources:
\end{enumerate}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Web-scraped data from various regional sources.
    \item
      Review datasets containing \textbf{\textbf{positive, negative, and
      neutral E}\textbf{lectric car}\textbf{ keywords}}.
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \textbf{\textbf{Positive Words}} (e.g., \emph{efficient, clean,
      fast}).
    \item
      \textbf{\textbf{Negative Words}} (e.g., \emph{expensive, slow
      charging, limited range}).
    \item
      \textbf{\textbf{Neutral Words}} (e.g., \emph{battery, motor,
      charging station}).
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}

  Data Collection:
\end{itemize}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Processed using \textbf{\textbf{custom dataset loaders} }that
      tokenize and store keywords.
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}

  \begin{enumerate}

\end{enumerate}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  Tokenization: Used \textbf{\textbf{AutoTokenizer}} from Hugging Face
  to convert words into numerical input for the model.
\item
  Labeling:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{\textbf{Positive Sentiment = 2}}
  \item
    \textbf{\textbf{Neutral Sentiment = 1}}
  \item
    \textbf{\textbf{Negative Sentiment = 0}}
  \end{itemize}
\item
  Fine-tuning:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    There is limited resource available so the model is fine tuned to
    understand the English language sentiments was used to fine-tune a
    \textbf{\textbf{BERT-based model}} to classify e-cars sentiments.
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  Metrics:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Accuracy (80\%): \textbf{The model correctly classified 80\%
    of sentiment-related words.}}
  \item
    \textbf{Precision (90\%)}: When the model predicted a sentiment, it
    was correct 90\% of the time, minimizing false positives.
  \item
    \textbf{Recall (80\%)}: The model identified 80\% of actual
    sentiment words, though some were misclassified as neutral.
  \item
    \textbf{F1-Score (80\%)}: A balanced performance between precision
    and recall, ensuring reliable sentiment classification.
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

  \tightlist
  \item
    \protect\hypertarget{anchor-394}{}{}Survey 
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

The survey section focuses on understanding consumer perspectives on
electric vehicles (EVs) by generating structured questions and analyzing
responses. It ensures that key topics such as charging infrastructure,
cost, and environmental impact are captured effectively.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Survey Generation
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Survey generation involves identifying key features related to electric
car adoption and formulating meaningful questions to gather consumer
insights.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \item
      \begin{enumerate}
      \def\labelenumiv{\arabic{enumiv}.}
      \tightlist
      \item
        Feature Extraction
      \end{enumerate}
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Feature extraction plays a crucial role in identifying relevant aspects
of EV adoption. It involves analyzing textual data from sources such as
reports, articles, and user reviews to extract essential topics. These
topics help in structuring questions that align with consumer concerns,
such as charging accessibility, battery performance, and
cost-effectiveness.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \item
      \begin{enumerate}
      \def\labelenumiv{\arabic{enumiv}.}
      \tightlist
      \item
        Using Transformer
      \end{enumerate}
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Transformer-based models, particularly T5, are utilized to generate
survey questions. The model takes extracted features as input and
converts them into structured questions. The summarization module
condenses large textual data into key insights, which are then processed
by a question generation model. This automated approach ensures that the
questions are comprehensive and aligned with the latest trends in e-cars
adoption.

This survey explores key factors influencing e-cars adoption, including
motivators and concerns.

Accelerators:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Environmental Benefits -- Importance of reducing carbon emissions.
\item
  Lower Running Costs -- Impact of fuel and maintenance savings.
\item
  Government Incentives -- Influence of tax credits and subsidies.
\item
  Technology Improvements -- Effect of battery and charging
  advancements.
\item
  Charging Infrastructure -- Availability of charging stations.
\end{itemize}

Resistance:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  High Initial Cost -- Concern over upfront expenses.
\item
  Limited Charging Access -- Accessibility of charging stations.
\item
  Charging Time -- Impact of long charging durations.
\item
  Battery Life \& Costs -- Worries about battery longevity and
  replacement expenses.
\item
  Range Anxiety -- Fear of running out of charge before reaching a
  station.
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \begin{enumerate}
  \def\labelenumii{\arabic{enumii}.}
  \item
    \begin{enumerate}
    \def\labelenumiii{\arabic{enumiii}.}
    \item
      Survey Response Analysis
    \end{enumerate}
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}

Once responses are collected, sentiment analysis is applied to determine
public opinion. Responses are classified into positive, neutral, and
negative categories, allowing for a clear understanding of consumer
sentiment. The analysis provides insights into consumer concerns,
preferences, and potential barriers to EV adoption.

Visualization techniques, such as pie charts and bar graphs, help in
interpreting the data effectively. Additionally, comparisons across
different regions or demographics can highlight variations in
perception, aiding policymakers and businesses in decision-making.
